# Thai Holy Basil Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Thai | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 1 large handful | basil leaves (Thai holy basil if you can find it)
- 1 | onion, sliced
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the oyster sauce, fish sauce, sugar and 3 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the onion with the garlic and chilies for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Turn off the heat and stir in the basil until it wilts.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Holy Basil Chicken Breast Stir-Fry
Main | Thai | 15 | 12 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 1 large handful | basil leaves (Thai holy basil if you can find it)
- 1 | onion, sliced
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken breast into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the oyster sauce, fish sauce, sugar and 3 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken breast in two batches, about 3 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the onion with the garlic and chilies for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken breast with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Turn off the heat and stir in the basil until it wilts.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Cashew Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Thai | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 60 g | cashews, toasted
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced
- 4 | spring onions, cut into lengths
- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the oyster sauce, soy sauce, sugar and 3 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the pepper and the white parts of the spring onions with the garlic and chilies for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Toss in the cashews and green spring onion.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Cashew Chicken Breast Stir-Fry
Main | Thai | 15 | 12 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 60 g | cashews, toasted
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced
- 4 | spring onions, cut into lengths
- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken breast into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the oyster sauce, soy sauce, sugar and 3 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken breast in two batches, about 3 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the pepper and the white parts of the spring onions with the garlic and chilies for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken breast with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Toss in the cashews and green spring onion.

# Thai Ginger Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Thai | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 5 cm | ginger, cut into thin matchsticks
- 150 g | mushrooms, sliced
- 1 | onion, sliced
- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the oyster sauce, soy sauce, fish sauce and 3 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the ginger, onion and mushrooms with the garlic and chilies for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Scatter with coriander if you have it.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Ginger Chicken Breast Stir-Fry
Main | Thai | 15 | 12 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 5 cm | ginger, cut into thin matchsticks
- 150 g | mushrooms, sliced
- 1 | onion, sliced
- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken breast into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the oyster sauce, soy sauce, fish sauce and 3 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken breast in two batches, about 3 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the ginger, onion and mushrooms with the garlic and chilies for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken breast with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Scatter with coriander if you have it.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Massaman Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Thai | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 tbsp | massaman curry paste
- 400 ml | coconut milk
- 2 | potatoes, cubed
- 1 | onion, cut into wedges
- 50 g | peanuts, roasted
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 | lime, juiced
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the curry paste.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic and chilies for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the coconut milk, potatoes, onion and 300 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the fish sauce, sugar and lime juice and scatter with peanuts. Taste for salt.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Massaman Chicken Drumsticks Curry
Main | Thai | 20 | 68 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 3 tbsp | massaman curry paste
- 400 ml | coconut milk
- 2 | potatoes, cubed
- 1 | onion, cut into wedges
- 50 g | peanuts, roasted
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 | lime, juiced
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken drumsticks dry and season well with salt and the curry paste.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken drumsticks in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic and chilies for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the coconut milk, potatoes, onion and 300 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the fish sauce, sugar and lime juice and scatter with peanuts. Taste for salt.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Red Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Thai | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 tbsp | red curry paste
- 400 ml | coconut milk
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced
- 200 g | green beans, trimmed
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tsp | brown sugar
- 1 handful | basil leaves
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the curry paste.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic and chilies for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the coconut milk and 200 ml water, adding the pepper and beans for the last 10 minutes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Season with the fish sauce and sugar and stir in the basil. Taste for salt.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Green Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Thai | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 tbsp | green curry paste
- 400 ml | coconut milk
- 1 | zucchini, sliced
- 1 | eggplant, cubed
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tsp | brown sugar
- 4 | lime leaves (optional)
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the curry paste.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic and chilies for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the coconut milk, lime leaves and 200 ml water, adding the eggplant and zucchini for the last 15 minutes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Season with the fish sauce and sugar. Taste for salt.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Green Chicken Drumsticks Curry

- 3 tbsp | green curry paste
- 400 ml | coconut milk

- 1 | eggplant, cubed

# Thai Lemongrass Chicken Thigh Traybake
Main | Thai | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 | lemongrass stalks, finely chopped
- 2 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 handful | coriander
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the lemongrass, garlic, fish sauce, sugar and soy sauce with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Spread on a lined tray in one layer.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Scatter with coriander and serve with lime wedges.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Lemongrass Chicken Breast Traybake
Main | Thai | 15 | 27 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 2 | lemongrass stalks, finely chopped
- 2 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 handful | coriander
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the lemongrass, garlic, fish sauce, sugar and soy sauce with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken breast and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to 4 hours in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Spread on a lined tray in one layer.
> Roast for about 22 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Scatter with coriander and serve with lime wedges.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Lemongrass Chicken Drumsticks Traybake
Main | Thai | 15 | 45 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 2 | lemongrass stalks, finely chopped
- 2 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 handful | coriander
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the lemongrass, garlic, fish sauce, sugar and soy sauce with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken drumsticks and leave to marinate 1 hour or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 210 °C (190 °C fan). Spread on a lined tray in one layer.
> Roast for about 40 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Scatter with coriander and serve with lime wedges.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Grilled Thai Gai Yang-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Thai | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 tsp | ground coriander
- 1 tsp | ground white pepper
- 4 tbsp | sweet chili sauce
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the fish sauce, soy sauce, sugar, coriander, pepper and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Spread on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with sweet chili sauce for dipping.

# Grilled Thai Gai Yang-Style Chicken Breast
Main | Thai | 15 | 27 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 2 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 tsp | ground coriander
- 1 tsp | ground white pepper
- 4 tbsp | sweet chili sauce
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the fish sauce, soy sauce, sugar, coriander, pepper and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken breast and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to 4 hours in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Spread on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 22 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with sweet chili sauce for dipping.

# Grilled Thai Gai Yang-Style Chicken Drumsticks
Main | Thai | 15 | 45 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 2 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 tsp | ground coriander
- 1 tsp | ground white pepper
- 4 tbsp | sweet chili sauce
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the fish sauce, soy sauce, sugar, coriander, pepper and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken drumsticks and leave to marinate 1 hour or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 210 °C (190 °C fan). Spread on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 40 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with sweet chili sauce for dipping.

# Thai Garlic Pepper Chicken Thigh
Main | Thai | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 1 tsp | ground white pepper
- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the white pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the garlic and chilies for 1 minute, then the garlic for 1 minute, then add the oyster sauce, fish sauce, sugar and 4 tbsp water.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Spoon the glossy garlic sauce over.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Garlic Pepper Chicken Breast
Main | Thai | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 1 tsp | ground white pepper
- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and the white pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the garlic and chilies for 1 minute, then the garlic for 1 minute, then add the oyster sauce, fish sauce, sugar and 4 tbsp water.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Spoon the glossy garlic sauce over.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Larb Chicken Lettuce Cups
Main | Thai | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 2 tbsp | fish sauce
- 2 tbsp | rice, toasted and ground
- 1 | red onion, thinly sliced
- 1 handful | mint leaves
- 1 head | lettuce, leaves separated
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the garlic and chilies for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the garlic and chilies and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the lime juice and fish sauce.
> Toss with the onion, mint and toasted rice powder and spoon into lettuce leaves.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Thai Basil Chicken Stir-Fry
Main | Thai | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 1 large handful | basil leaves
- 4 | eggs, fried
- 4 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 2 | red chilies, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the garlic and chilies for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the garlic and chilies and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the oyster sauce, fish sauce, sugar and 3 tbsp water.
> Stir in the basil and serve each portion topped with a fried egg.
> Serve with jasmine rice.

# Chinese Black Bean Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Chinese | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tbsp | black bean sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 1 | green bell pepper, sliced
- 1 | onion, sliced
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the black bean sauce, soy sauce, sugar and 4 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the pepper and onion with the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Scatter with the green spring onions.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Kung Pao Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Chinese | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 6 | dried red chilies
- 1 tsp | Sichuan peppercorns, crushed
- 60 g | peanuts, roasted
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | rice vinegar
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the soy sauce, vinegar, sugar, cornstarch and 3 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the dried chilies and Sichuan pepper for 30 seconds, then with the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Toss in the peanuts and green spring onions.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Broccoli and Oyster Sauce Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Chinese | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 300 g | broccoli, in small florets
- 3 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | sesame oil
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the oyster sauce, soy sauce and 3 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the broccoli with 3 tbsp water (cover 2 minutes to steam) and with the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Finish with the sesame oil.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Broccoli and Oyster Sauce Chicken Breast Stir-Fry
Main | Chinese | 15 | 12 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 300 g | broccoli, in small florets
- 3 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | sesame oil
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken breast into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the oyster sauce, soy sauce and 3 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken breast in two batches, about 3 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the broccoli with 3 tbsp water (cover 2 minutes to steam) and with the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken breast with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Finish with the sesame oil.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Sweet and Sour Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Chinese | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 1 | red bell pepper, in chunks
- 1 | onion, in chunks
- 200 g | pineapple chunks
- 3 tbsp | ketchup
- 2 tbsp | rice vinegar
- 2 tbsp | sugar
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the ketchup, vinegar, sugar, soy sauce, cornstarch and 4 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the pepper, onion and pineapple with the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Toss until everything is coated in the bright sauce.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Sweet and Sour Chicken Breast Stir-Fry
Main | Chinese | 15 | 12 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 1 | red bell pepper, in chunks
- 1 | onion, in chunks
- 200 g | pineapple chunks
- 3 tbsp | ketchup
- 2 tbsp | rice vinegar

- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced

> For the sauce, stir together the ketchup, vinegar, sugar, soy sauce, cornstarch and 4 tbsp water.

# Chinese Red-Braised Chicken Thighs Hong Shao
Main | Chinese | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 4 tbsp | soy sauce (1 tbsp dark if you have it)
- 3 tbsp | Shaoxing wine (or dry sherry)
- 30 g | brown sugar (or rock sugar)
- 2 | star anise
- 1 | cinnamon stick
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the star anise and cinnamon.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the soy sauces, wine, sugar and water to just cover, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Uncover for the last 15 minutes and boil until the sauce is sticky. Taste for salt.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Red-Braised Chicken Drumsticks Hong Shao
Main | Chinese | 20 | 68 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 4 tbsp | soy sauce (1 tbsp dark if you have it)
- 3 tbsp | Shaoxing wine (or dry sherry)
- 30 g | brown sugar (or rock sugar)
- 2 | star anise
- 1 | cinnamon stick
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken drumsticks dry and season well with salt and the star anise and cinnamon.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken drumsticks in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the soy sauces, wine, sugar and water to just cover, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Uncover for the last 15 minutes and boil until the sauce is sticky. Taste for salt.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Five-Spice Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | Chinese | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 tsp | five-spice powder
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 2 tbsp | hoisin sauce
- 2 tbsp | Shaoxing wine (or dry sherry)
- 300 g | carrots, in chunks
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the five-spice.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the soy sauce, hoisin, wine, carrots and 400 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Reduce the sauce until it coats the meat. Taste for salt.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Roast Chinese Char Siu-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Chinese | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 tbsp | hoisin sauce
- 2 tbsp | honey
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | five-spice powder
- 1 tbsp | Shaoxing wine (or dry sherry)
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the hoisin, honey, soy sauce, five-spice, wine and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Set on a rack over a lined tray and brush with more marinade halfway.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Slice and spoon over the pan juices.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Roast Chinese Char Siu-Style Chicken Breast
Main | Chinese | 15 | 27 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 3 tbsp | hoisin sauce
- 2 tbsp | honey
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | five-spice powder
- 1 tbsp | Shaoxing wine (or dry sherry)

- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil

> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Set on a rack over a lined tray and brush with more marinade halfway.
> Roast for about 22 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Slice and spoon over the pan juices.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Ginger Scallion Chicken Thigh
Main | Chinese | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | Shaoxing wine (or dry sherry)
- 1 tsp | sesame oil
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and white pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 1 minute, then the ginger and spring onions for 1 minute, then add the soy sauce, wine and 4 tbsp water.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Drizzle with the sesame oil.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Ginger Scallion Chicken Breast
Main | Chinese | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | Shaoxing wine (or dry sherry)
- 1 tsp | sesame oil
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and white pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 1 minute, then the ginger and spring onions for 1 minute, then add the soy sauce, wine and 4 tbsp water.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Drizzle with the sesame oil.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Mapo-Style Chicken Tofu
Main | Chinese | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 400 g | silken tofu, cubed
- 2 tbsp | doubanjiang (chili bean paste)
- 1 tsp | Sichuan peppercorns, ground
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 250 ml | chicken stock
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the chili bean paste and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the stock, soy sauce and tofu; simmer 5 minutes, then thicken with the cornstarch mixed with 2 tbsp water.
> Sprinkle with the ground Sichuan pepper and green spring onions.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Chinese Lion's Head Chicken Meatballs
Main | Chinese | 20 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 1 | egg
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | Shaoxing wine (or dry sherry)
- 2 tbsp | cornstarch
- 500 ml | chicken stock
- 400 g | napa cabbage, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, chopped
- 3 cm | ginger, chopped
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the chicken with the egg, soy sauce, wine, cornstarch, ginger and spring onion whites and 1 tsp salt; with wet hands shape into 16 balls.
> Brown in 2 tbsp oil over medium-high heat in batches, about 6 minutes, turning; they finish cooking in the sauce.
> Cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions gently in the same pan for 5 minutes.
> Add the stock and cabbage; cover and simmer 20 minutes, until the meat is no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve the meatballs in bowls with the cabbage and broth.

# Sichuan Dan Dan-Style Chicken Noodles
Main | Chinese | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 400 g | wheat noodles
- 2 tbsp | sesame paste (or tahini)
- 2 tbsp | chili oil
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | Sichuan peppercorns, ground
- 200 g | bok choy
- 4 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 3 cm | ginger, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the garlic and ginger for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the ginger and garlic and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the soy sauce and fry until the meat is crisp.
> Divide the sesame paste, chili oil and Sichuan pepper between bowls, add the boiled noodles and bok choy and top with the crispy meat.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Japanese Ginger Chicken Thigh Shogayaki
Main | Japanese | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 2 tbsp | mirin
- 1 tbsp | sake (or dry sherry)
- 1 | onion, sliced
- 200 g | cabbage, finely shredded
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the soy sauce, mirin, sake and grated ginger.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the onion with the garlic and ginger for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Serve with the raw shredded cabbage.

# Japanese Yakisoba Chicken Thigh Noodles

- 1 | carrot, cut into matchsticks

- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | ketchup
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 1 tsp | cornstarch

# Japanese Yakisoba Chicken Breast Noodles
Main | Japanese | 15 | 12 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish

- 4 tbsp | Worcestershire sauce

- 2 | garlic cloves, grated

> For the sauce, stir together the Worcestershire sauce, oyster sauce and ketchup.

# Japanese Nikujaga-Style Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Japanese | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 500 g | potatoes, in chunks
- 1 | onion, in wedges
- 2 | carrots, in chunks
- 4 tbsp | soy sauce
- 3 tbsp | mirin
- 2 tbsp | sugar
- 500 ml | dashi (or water)
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the onion.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the dashi, soy sauce, mirin, sugar, potatoes and carrots, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Let it sit 10 minutes so the potatoes soak up the sauce. Taste for salt.
> Serve with steamed short-grain rice.

# Japanese Nikujaga-Style Chicken Drumsticks Stew
Main | Japanese | 20 | 68 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 500 g | potatoes, in chunks
- 1 | onion, in wedges
- 2 | carrots, in chunks
- 4 tbsp | soy sauce
- 3 tbsp | mirin
- 2 tbsp | sugar
- 500 ml | dashi (or water)
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken drumsticks dry and season well with salt and the onion.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken drumsticks in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the dashi, soy sauce, mirin, sugar, potatoes and carrots, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Let it sit 10 minutes so the potatoes soak up the sauce. Taste for salt.
> Serve with steamed short-grain rice.

# Japanese Japanese Curry Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Japanese | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 100 g | Japanese curry roux
- 2 | onions, sliced
- 2 | carrots, in chunks
- 2 | potatoes, in chunks
- 1 | apple, grated
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the onions until golden.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add 700 ml water, the carrots, potatoes and apple, then stir in the curry roux for the last 10 minutes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve over rice. Taste for salt.

# Japanese Miso Chicken Thigh Bake
Main | Japanese | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 tbsp | white miso
- 2 tbsp | mirin
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 1 tbsp | sake (or dry sherry)
- 1 tbsp | sesame seeds
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the miso, mirin, sugar and sake with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Spread on a lined tray.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Sprinkle with sesame seeds.
> Serve with steamed short-grain rice.

# Japanese Miso Chicken Breast Bake
Main | Japanese | 15 | 27 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 3 tbsp | white miso
- 2 tbsp | mirin
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 1 tbsp | sake (or dry sherry)
- 1 tbsp | sesame seeds
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the miso, mirin, sugar and sake with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken breast and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to 4 hours in the fridge.

> Roast for about 22 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Sprinkle with sesame seeds.
> Serve with steamed short-grain rice.

# Japanese Teriyaki Chicken Thigh
Main | Japanese | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 4 tbsp | soy sauce
- 3 tbsp | mirin
- 2 tbsp | sake (or dry sherry)
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 1 tbsp | sesame seeds
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and a pinch of salt only (the sauce is salty).
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the garlic and ginger for 1 minute, then the soy sauce, mirin, sake and sugar and let it bubble 2 minutes until syrupy.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Turn to coat and sprinkle with sesame seeds.
> Serve with steamed short-grain rice.

# Japanese Teriyaki Chicken Breast
Main | Japanese | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 4 tbsp | soy sauce
- 3 tbsp | mirin
- 2 tbsp | sake (or dry sherry)
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 1 tbsp | sesame seeds
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and a pinch of salt only (the sauce is salty).
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the garlic and ginger for 1 minute, then the soy sauce, mirin, sake and sugar and let it bubble 2 minutes until syrupy.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Turn to coat and sprinkle with sesame seeds.
> Serve with steamed short-grain rice.

# Japanese Butter Ponzu Chicken Thigh
Main | Japanese | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 4 tbsp | ponzu (or soy sauce with lemon juice)
- 30 g | butter
- 2 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the garlic and ginger for 1 minute, then the ponzu and butter and swirl until glossy.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Scatter with the spring onions.
> Serve with steamed short-grain rice.

# Japanese Tsukune-Style Chicken Patties
Main | Japanese | 20 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 1 | egg
- 2 tbsp | panko breadcrumbs
- 4 tbsp | soy sauce
- 3 tbsp | mirin
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 2 | spring onions, finely chopped
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the chicken with the egg, panko, spring onions, ginger and a pinch of salt and 1 tsp salt; with wet hands shape into 8 patties or logs.
> Brown in 2 tbsp oil over medium-high heat in batches, about 8 minutes, turning, until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir together the soy sauce, mirin and sugar and bubble until it coats the patties.
> Serve glazed, with rice.

# Japanese Soboro Chicken Rice Bowls
Main | Japanese | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 2 tbsp | mirin
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 4 | eggs, beaten
- 150 g | green peas
- 2 | garlic cloves, grated
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the garlic and ginger for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the ginger and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the soy sauce, mirin and sugar and cook until dry and crumbly.
> Scramble the eggs into fine curds separately, then top bowls of rice with the meat, egg and peas.
> Serve with steamed short-grain rice.

# Korean Bulgogi-Style Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Korean | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish

# Korean Galbi Jjim-Style Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | Korean | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 5 tbsp | soy sauce
- 2 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 | pear (or apple), grated
- 2 | carrots, in chunks
- 300 g | daikon radish, in chunks
- 6 | dried shiitake mushrooms, soaked
- 4 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.

> Reduce the sauce until glossy and sprinkle with sesame seeds. Taste for salt.

> Lower the heat and cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the soy sauce, sugar, grated pear, mushrooms and water to just cover, adding the carrot and daikon for the last 40 minutes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Reduce the sauce until glossy and sprinkle with sesame seeds. Taste for salt.
> Serve with steamed rice and kimchi.

# Korean Dakdoritang-Style Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Korean | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 tbsp | gochujang
- 2 tbsp | gochugaru
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 3 | potatoes, in chunks
- 2 | carrots, in chunks
- 1 | onion, in wedges
- 4 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the gochujang and chili flakes.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the soy sauce, sugar, potatoes, carrots, onion and 600 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Scatter with the green spring onions. Taste for salt.
> Serve with steamed rice and kimchi.

# Korean Gochujang Chicken Thigh Traybake
Main | Korean | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 tbsp | gochujang
- 2 tbsp | honey
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | sesame oil
- 1 tbsp | rice vinegar
- 4 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the gochujang, honey, soy sauce, sesame oil, vinegar and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Spread on a lined tray and turn once.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Sprinkle with the green spring onions and serve with lettuce leaves for wrapping.
> Serve with steamed rice and kimchi.

# Korean Soy Garlic Chicken Thigh
Main | Korean | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 2 tbsp | honey
- 1 tbsp | rice vinegar
- 1 tsp | gochugaru
- 4 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 1 minute, then the garlic for 1 minute, then add the soy sauce, honey, vinegar and chili flakes and bubble until sticky.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Turn to coat.
> Serve with steamed rice and kimchi.

# Korean Soy Garlic Chicken Breast
Main | Korean | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 2 tbsp | honey
- 1 tbsp | rice vinegar
- 1 tsp | gochugaru
- 4 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 1 minute, then the garlic for 1 minute, then add the soy sauce, honey, vinegar and chili flakes and bubble until sticky.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Turn to coat.
> Serve with steamed rice and kimchi.

# Korean Gochujang Chicken Rice Bowls
Main | Korean | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 2 tbsp | gochujang
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 1 tbsp | sesame oil
- 4 | eggs, fried
- 1 | cucumber, cut into matchsticks
- 200 g | spinach, wilted
- 4 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 4 | spring onions, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the garlic, ginger and white parts of the spring onions for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the garlic and ginger and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the gochujang, soy sauce, sugar and sesame oil.
> Spoon over rice with the cucumber, spinach and a fried egg.
> Serve with steamed rice and kimchi.

# Vietnamese Lemongrass Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Vietnamese | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 | lemongrass stalks, finely chopped
- 2 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 1 | red chili, sliced
- 1 | onion, sliced
- 1 handful | coriander
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 | shallots, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the fish sauce, sugar and 2 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the lemongrass, chili and onion with the garlic and shallots for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Top with the coriander.
> Serve with rice or rice noodles with herbs.

# Vietnamese Shaking Chicken Thigh Bo Luc Lac-Style
Main | Vietnamese | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish

- 2 tbsp | oyster sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 1 | red onion, in wedges
- 100 g | watercress (or salad leaves)
- 2 | tomatoes, sliced
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 | shallots, sliced
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the oyster sauce, soy sauce, fish sauce and sugar.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.

# Vietnamese Caramel Chicken Thighs Kho
Main | Vietnamese | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 tbsp | sugar
- 3 tbsp | fish sauce
- 400 ml | coconut water (or water)
- 1 tsp | black pepper
- 4 | eggs, boiled and peeled (optional)
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 | shallots, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the sugar, cooked dry in the pot until dark amber, then.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic and shallots for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the fish sauce, coconut water, pepper and eggs, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Reduce until the sauce is dark and sticky. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice or rice noodles with herbs.

# Vietnamese Bun Cha-Style Chicken Patties
Main | Vietnamese | 20 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 2 tbsp | fish sauce
- 1 tbsp | sugar
- 300 g | rice noodles
- 1 head | lettuce
- 1 handful | mint leaves
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 | shallots, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the chicken with half the fish sauce, half the sugar, the garlic and shallots and 1 tsp salt; with wet hands shape into 8 patties or logs.
> Brown in 2 tbsp oil over medium-high heat in batches, about 8 minutes, turning, until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Cook the garlic and shallots gently in the same pan for 5 minutes.
> Stir together the rest of the fish sauce and sugar with the lime juice and 150 ml warm water as a dipping sauce.
> Serve with the noodles, lettuce and herbs, dipping into the sauce.

# Indian Butter Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Indian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 150 ml | heavy cream
- 40 g | butter
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | chili powder
- 1 tsp | dried fenugreek leaves (optional)
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the garam masala, cumin and chili.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onions, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the tomatoes and 150 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the cream, butter and fenugreek and simmer 5 minutes. Taste for salt.
> Serve with basmati rice or naan.

# Indian Rogan Josh-Style Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Indian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 150 g | plain yogurt
- 2 tsp | Kashmiri chili powder (or paprika)
- 1 tsp | ground fennel
- 1 tsp | ground ginger
- 4 | cardamom pods
- 1 | cinnamon stick
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the chili, fennel, ginger, cardamom and cinnamon.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onions, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the yogurt, a spoonful at a time, and 400 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Simmer uncovered until the sauce is thick and red. Taste for salt.
> Serve with basmati rice or naan.

# Indian Rogan Josh-Style Chicken Drumsticks Curry
Main | Indian | 20 | 68 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 150 g | plain yogurt
- 2 tsp | Kashmiri chili powder (or paprika)
- 1 tsp | ground fennel
- 1 tsp | ground ginger
- 4 | cardamom pods
- 1 | cinnamon stick
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken drumsticks dry and season well with salt and the chili, fennel, ginger, cardamom and cinnamon.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken drumsticks in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onions, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the yogurt, a spoonful at a time, and 400 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Simmer uncovered until the sauce is thick and red. Taste for salt.
> Serve with basmati rice or naan.

# Indian Korma Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Indian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 60 g | ground almonds
- 150 g | plain yogurt
- 200 ml | coconut milk
- 1 tsp | ground cardamom
- 1 tsp | ground coriander
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the cardamom, coriander and turmeric.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onions, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the yogurt, almonds, coconut milk and 200 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Scatter with toasted almonds. Taste for salt.
> Serve with basmati rice or naan.

# Indian Vindaloo-Style Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Indian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 tbsp | white vinegar
- 2 tsp | Kashmiri chili powder (or paprika)
- 1 tsp | cayenne
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | mustard seeds
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the chili, cayenne, cumin and mustard seeds.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onions, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the vinegar, tomatoes and 250 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Let it rest 10 minutes; it tastes even better the next day. Taste for salt.
> Serve with basmati rice or naan.

# Indian Saag Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Indian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 400 g | spinach, chopped
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 100 ml | heavy cream
- 1 | green chili
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the garam masala, cumin, turmeric and chili.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onions, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add 300 ml water, then the spinach for the last 10 minutes; blend half the sauce if you like it smooth, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the cream. Taste for salt.
> Serve with basmati rice or naan.

# Roast Indian Tandoori-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Indian | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 200 g | plain yogurt
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 2 tsp | Kashmiri chili powder (or paprika)
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 | lemon, juiced
- 1 | red onion, sliced into rings
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the yogurt, garlic, ginger, spices and lemon juice with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with the raw onion rings and lemon wedges.

# Roast Indian Tandoori-Style Chicken Breast
Main | Indian | 15 | 27 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 200 g | plain yogurt
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 2 tsp | Kashmiri chili powder (or paprika)
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 | lemon, juiced
- 1 | red onion, sliced into rings
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the yogurt, garlic, ginger, spices and lemon juice with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken breast and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to 4 hours in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 22 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with the raw onion rings and lemon wedges.

# Roast Indian Tandoori-Style Chicken Drumsticks
Main | Indian | 15 | 45 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 200 g | plain yogurt
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 2 tsp | Kashmiri chili powder (or paprika)
- 1 tsp | ground cumin

- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated

# Indian Tikka Chicken Thigh Skewers
Main | Indian | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 200 g | plain yogurt
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 1 tsp | chili powder
- 2 | bell peppers, in chunks
- 1 | red onion, in chunks
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the yogurt, garlic, ginger and spices with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Thread on skewers with the peppers and onion and lay on a lined tray.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with mint yogurt.

# Indian Masala Chicken Thigh
Main | Indian | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 1 tsp | chili powder
- 3 | tomatoes, chopped
- 1 handful | coriander
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the garam masala, turmeric and chili.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onions, garlic and ginger for 1 minute, then the onions, garlic and ginger for 5 minutes, then the tomatoes and 4 tbsp water until jammy.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Scatter with coriander.
> Serve with basmati rice or naan.

# Indian Masala Chicken Breast
Main | Indian | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 1 tsp | chili powder
- 3 | tomatoes, chopped
- 1 handful | coriander
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and the garam masala, turmeric and chili.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onions, garlic and ginger for 1 minute, then the onions, garlic and ginger for 5 minutes, then the tomatoes and 4 tbsp water until jammy.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Scatter with coriander.
> Serve with basmati rice or naan.

# Indian Keema Chicken Mince
Main | Indian | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 200 g | green peas
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the onions, garlic and ginger for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the garam masala, cumin and turmeric and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the tomatoes, then the peas for the last 5 minutes.
> Scatter with coriander.
> Serve with basmati rice or naan.

# Indian Seekh Kebab-Style Chicken Kebabs
Main | Indian | 20 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 2 tsp | garam masala
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | chili powder
- 1 handful | coriander, chopped
- 1 | green chili, finely chopped
- 150 g | plain yogurt
- 1 handful | mint leaves
- 2 | onions, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the chicken with the garam masala, cumin, chili powder, coriander, green chili, garlic and ginger and 1 tsp salt; with wet hands shape into 8 patties or logs.
> Brown in 2 tbsp oil over medium-high heat in batches, about 8 minutes, turning, until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Cook the onions, garlic and ginger gently in the same pan for 5 minutes.
> Stir together the yogurt mixed with chopped mint.
> Serve with the mint yogurt and sliced onion.

# Sri Lankan Black Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Sri Lankan | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 tbsp | roasted curry powder
- 1 tsp | black pepper
- 2 tbsp | tamarind paste
- 1 | cinnamon stick
- 200 ml | coconut milk
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 10 | curry leaves (optional)
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the curry powder, pepper and cinnamon.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, garlic, ginger and curry leaves for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the tamarind and 400 ml water, then the coconut milk for the last 20 minutes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Simmer until dark and thick. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice.

# Sri Lankan Devilled Chicken Thigh
Main | Sri Lankan | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 1 | onion, in chunks
- 1 | green bell pepper, in chunks
- 3 tbsp | ketchup
- 1 tbsp | chili sauce
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tsp | chili flakes
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 10 | curry leaves (optional)
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the chili flakes.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion, garlic, ginger and curry leaves for 1 minute, then the onion and pepper for 3 minutes, then the ketchup, chili sauce, soy sauce and 3 tbsp water.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Toss to coat.
> Serve with rice.

# Indonesian Rendang-Style Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | Indonesian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 | lemongrass stalks, bruised
- 3 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tsp | ground coriander
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 400 ml | coconut milk
- 40 g | desiccated coconut, toasted
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 4 | shallots, sliced
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 | red chilies, chopped
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the ginger, coriander and turmeric.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the shallots, garlic and chilies for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the coconut milk, lemongrass, sugar and 200 ml water; uncover for the last hour and stir often until almost dry, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the toasted coconut and fry a few minutes more until dark. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice.

> Add the coconut milk, lemongrass, lime leaves and 200 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Scatter with fried shallots if you have them. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice.

# Indonesian Kecap Manis Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Indonesian | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 4 tbsp | kecap manis (sweet soy sauce)
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 200 g | green beans, cut short
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced
- 4 | shallots, sliced
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 | red chilies, chopped
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the kecap manis, soy sauce and 2 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the green beans and pepper with the shallots, garlic and chilies for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Toss until sticky.
> Serve with rice.

# Indonesian Kecap Manis Chicken Breast Stir-Fry

- 200 g | green beans, cut short
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced

> For the sauce, stir together the kecap manis, soy sauce and 2 tbsp water.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken breast in two batches, about 3 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the green beans and pepper with the shallots, garlic and chilies for 2 to 3 minutes.

# Indonesian Satay Chicken Thigh Skewers
Main | Indonesian | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 tbsp | kecap manis (sweet soy sauce)
- 1 tsp | ground coriander
- 100 g | peanut butter
- 200 ml | coconut milk
- 1 | lime, juiced
- 1 tsp | chili flakes
- 4 | shallots, sliced
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 | red chilies, chopped
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the kecap manis, coriander, shallots and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Thread on skewers and lay on a lined tray; meanwhile simmer the peanut butter, coconut milk, lime juice and chili flakes into a sauce.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with the peanut sauce.

# Indonesian Satay Chicken Breast Skewers

# Indonesian Satay Chicken Drumsticks Skewers

# Indonesian Nasi Goreng-Style Chicken Fried Rice
Main | Indonesian | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 600 g | rice, cooked and cold
- 3 tbsp | kecap manis (sweet soy sauce)
- 1 tbsp | soy sauce
- 4 | eggs, fried
- 1 | cucumber, sliced
- 4 | shallots, sliced
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 | red chilies, chopped
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the shallots, garlic and chilies for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the shallots, garlic and chilies and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the cold rice, kecap manis and soy sauce and fry until hot and smoky.
> Serve topped with a fried egg and cucumber on the side.

# Malaysian Kapitan-Style Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Malaysian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 | lemongrass stalks, finely chopped
- 2 tbsp | chili garlic sauce
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 400 ml | coconut milk
- 1 tbsp | tamarind paste
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 4 | shallots, sliced
- 3 | garlic cloves
- 2 cm | ginger
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the chili garlic sauce, lemongrass and turmeric.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the shallots, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the coconut milk, tamarind and 200 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Season with sugar and salt. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice.

# Filipino Adobo Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | Filipino | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 120 ml | soy sauce
- 120 ml | white vinegar
- 3 | bay leaves
- 1 tsp | black peppercorns
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 6 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the peppercorns and bay leaves.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the soy sauce, vinegar, sugar and 200 ml water; let it boil 2 minutes before stirring so the vinegar mellows, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Reduce the sauce until it coats the meat. Taste for salt.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Filipino Caldereta-Style Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Filipino | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 400 g | tomato sauce
- 2 | potatoes, in chunks
- 2 | carrots, in chunks
- 1 | red bell pepper, in chunks
- 2 tbsp | liver pate (or peanut butter)
- 50 g | green olives
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 6 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the tomato sauce and 300 ml water, adding the potatoes and carrots for the last 30 minutes and the pepper for the last 10, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the pate and olives. Taste for salt.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Filipino Bistek-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Filipino | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 4 tbsp | soy sauce
- 2 | lemons, juiced
- 2 | onions, sliced into rings
- 6 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the onion rings for 3 minutes, then the soy sauce, lemon juice and 4 tbsp water.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Spoon the onions and sauce over.
> Serve with steamed rice.

# Filipino Bistek-Style Chicken Breast

- 2 | onions, sliced into rings
- 6 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil

> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the onion rings for 3 minutes, then the soy sauce, lemon juice and 4 tbsp water.

> Serve with steamed rice.
