> Add the tomatoes, chipotles and 200 ml water; shred the meat at the end, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve in tortillas with avocado and crema. Taste for salt.

# Mexican Red Chile Chicken Thighs Guisado
Main | Mexican | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 4 | dried guajillo chilies, seeded and soaked
- 2 | dried ancho chilies, seeded and soaked
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 500 ml | chicken stock
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the cumin and oregano.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the chilies blended with the stock, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve with rice and beans. Taste for salt.

# Mexican Salsa Verde Chicken Thighs Guisado
Main | Mexican | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 500 g | tomatillos (or green salsa)
- 2 | jalapeños
- 1 large handful | coriander
- 300 ml | chicken stock
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the tomatillos, jalapeños and coriander blended with the stock, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Scatter with more coriander. Taste for salt.
> Serve with warm tortillas and rice.

# Mexican Barbacoa-Style Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | Mexican | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 | chipotle peppers in adobo
- 3 tbsp | cider vinegar
- 2 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 3 | bay leaves
- 1 | lime, juiced
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the cumin and oregano.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the chipotles blended with the vinegar and 300 ml water, plus the bay leaves; shred the meat at the end, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Squeeze over the lime and serve in tortillas. Taste for salt.
> Serve with warm tortillas and rice.

# Roast Mexican Achiote Chicken Thigh
Main | Mexican | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 50 g | achiote paste (annatto paste)
- 2 | oranges, juiced
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 1 | red onion, sliced
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the achiote paste, citrus juice, oregano and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay in a roasting dish with the marinade.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with quick-pickled red onion.

# Roast Mexican Achiote Chicken Breast

- 1 tsp | dried oregano

- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil

# Grilled Mexican Carne Asada-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Mexican | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 1 | orange, juiced
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | chili powder
- 1 large handful | coriander, chopped
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the citrus juice, cumin, chili powder, coriander and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Slice thinly and serve in tortillas with salsa.
> Serve with warm tortillas and rice.

# Roast Mexican Al Pastor-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Mexican | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 | dried guajillo chilies, soaked
- 2 | chipotle peppers in adobo
- 100 ml | pineapple juice
- 2 tbsp | white vinegar
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 200 g | pineapple, in slices
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the soaked chilies, chipotles, pineapple juice, vinegar, cumin and garlic, blended with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay on a lined tray with the pineapple slices around it.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Chop with the pineapple and serve in tortillas.
> Serve with warm tortillas and rice.

# Mexican Fajita Chicken Thigh
Main | Mexican | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 | bell peppers, sliced
- 1 | onion, sliced
- 2 tsp | chili powder
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | smoked paprika
- 1 | lime, juiced
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the chili powder, cumin and paprika.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the peppers and onion until charred at the edges, then a splash of water.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Slice the meat, squeeze over the lime and pile into tortillas.
> Serve with warm tortillas and rice.

# Mexican Fajita Chicken Breast
Main | Mexican | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 2 | bell peppers, sliced
- 1 | onion, sliced
- 2 tsp | chili powder
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | smoked paprika
- 1 | lime, juiced
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and the chili powder, cumin and paprika.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the peppers and onion until charred at the edges, then a splash of water.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Slice the meat, squeeze over the lime and pile into tortillas.
> Serve with warm tortillas and rice.

# Mexican Mole-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Mexican | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tbsp | mole paste
- 250 ml | chicken stock
- 20 g | dark chocolate
- 1 tbsp | sesame seeds
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and ground cumin.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the mole paste and stock and simmer until thick, then melt in the chocolate.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Spoon the sauce over and sprinkle with sesame seeds.
> Serve with warm tortillas and rice.

# Mexican Picadillo Chicken Mince
Main | Mexican | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 2 | potatoes, diced small
- 2 | carrots, diced small
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the onion and garlic for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the cumin and oregano and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the tomatoes, potatoes, carrots and 150 ml water; simmer 15 minutes until tender.
> Serve with rice or in tortillas.

# Mexican Albondigas Chicken Meatballs
Main | Mexican | 20 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 1 | egg
- 40 g | rice, uncooked
- 1 handful | mint leaves, chopped
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 1 | chipotle pepper in adobo
- 750 ml | chicken stock
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the chicken with the egg, rice, mint and half the garlic and 1 tsp salt; with wet hands shape into 16 balls.
> Brown in 2 tbsp oil over medium-high heat in batches, about 6 minutes, turning; they finish cooking in the sauce.
> Cook the onion and garlic gently in the same pan for 5 minutes.
> Add the tomatoes blended with the chipotle, then the stock; simmer the meatballs 25 minutes, until the meat is no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve as a soupy stew.

# Tex-Mex Queso Chicken Thigh

- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil

# Tex-Mex Taco Chicken
Main | Tex-Mex | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 2 tbsp | chili powder
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | smoked paprika
- 200 g | tomato sauce
- 8 | taco shells
- 100 g | cheddar cheese, grated
- 1 head | lettuce, shredded
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the onion and garlic for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the chili powder, cumin and paprika and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).

# Tex-Mex Enchilada Chicken Bake
Main | Tex-Mex | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 2 tsp | chili powder
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 500 ml | enchilada sauce (or tomato passata with chili)

- 150 g | cheddar cheese, grated
- 400 g | black beans, drained
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil

> Add the chicken and the chili powder and cumin and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in half the sauce and the beans.
> Roll the filling in tortillas, cover with the rest of the sauce and cheese and bake at 200 °C for 20 minutes.
> Serve with rice and beans.

# Jamaican Curry Chicken Thighs Curry
Main | Jamaican | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 tbsp | curry powder (Jamaican-style)
- 1 tsp | ground allspice
- 1 tsp | dried thyme
- 2 | potatoes, in chunks
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves
- 4 | spring onions, chopped
- 1 | scotch bonnet pepper, seeded
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the curry powder, allspice and thyme.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, garlic, spring onions and scotch bonnet for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add 500 ml water and the potatoes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Simmer uncovered until thick. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice and peas.

# Jamaican Curry Chicken Drumsticks Curry
Main | Jamaican | 20 | 68 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 3 tbsp | curry powder (Jamaican-style)
- 1 tsp | ground allspice
- 1 tsp | dried thyme
- 2 | potatoes, in chunks
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves
- 4 | spring onions, chopped
- 1 | scotch bonnet pepper, seeded
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken drumsticks dry and season well with salt and the curry powder, allspice and thyme.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken drumsticks in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, garlic, spring onions and scotch bonnet for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add 500 ml water and the potatoes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Simmer uncovered until thick. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice and peas.

# Jamaican Brown Stew Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Jamaican | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 tbsp | browning sauce (or dark soy sauce)
- 1 tbsp | ketchup
- 1 tsp | ground allspice
- 1 tsp | dried thyme
- 2 | carrots, sliced
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves
- 4 | spring onions, chopped
- 1 | scotch bonnet pepper, seeded
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the allspice and thyme.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, garlic, spring onions and scotch bonnet for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the browning sauce, ketchup and 400 ml water, with the carrots and pepper for the last 15 minutes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve with rice. Taste for salt.

# Jamaican Brown Stew Chicken Drumsticks Stew
Main | Jamaican | 20 | 68 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 2 tbsp | browning sauce (or dark soy sauce)
- 1 tbsp | ketchup
- 1 tsp | ground allspice
- 1 tsp | dried thyme
- 2 | carrots, sliced
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves
- 4 | spring onions, chopped
- 1 | scotch bonnet pepper, seeded
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken drumsticks dry and season well with salt and the allspice and thyme.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken drumsticks in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, garlic, spring onions and scotch bonnet for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the browning sauce, ketchup and 400 ml water, with the carrots and pepper for the last 15 minutes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve with rice. Taste for salt.

# Roast Jamaican Jerk Chicken Thigh
Main | Jamaican | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tbsp | jerk seasoning (or paste)
- 1 tsp | ground allspice
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves
- 4 | spring onions, chopped
- 1 | scotch bonnet pepper, seeded
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the jerk seasoning, allspice, sugar, lime, soy sauce, spring onions, garlic and scotch bonnet blended with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with lime wedges.

# Roast Jamaican Jerk Chicken Breast
Main | Jamaican | 15 | 27 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 2 tbsp | jerk seasoning (or paste)
- 1 tsp | ground allspice
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves
- 4 | spring onions, chopped
- 1 | scotch bonnet pepper, seeded
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the jerk seasoning, allspice, sugar, lime, soy sauce, spring onions, garlic and scotch bonnet blended with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken breast and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to 4 hours in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 22 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with lime wedges.

# Roast Jamaican Jerk Chicken Drumsticks
Main | Jamaican | 15 | 45 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 2 tbsp | jerk seasoning (or paste)
- 1 tsp | ground allspice
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves
- 4 | spring onions, chopped
- 1 | scotch bonnet pepper, seeded
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the jerk seasoning, allspice, sugar, lime, soy sauce, spring onions, garlic and scotch bonnet blended with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken drumsticks and leave to marinate 1 hour or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 210 °C (190 °C fan). Lay on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 40 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with lime wedges.

# Jamaican Escovitch Chicken Thigh
Main | Jamaican | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 1 | carrot, cut into thin strips
- 1 | red bell pepper, cut into thin strips
- 120 ml | white vinegar
- 1 tsp | ground allspice
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves
- 4 | spring onions, chopped
- 1 | scotch bonnet pepper, seeded
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the allspice.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion, garlic, spring onions and scotch bonnet for 1 minute, then the onion, carrot, pepper and scotch bonnet for 2 minutes, then the vinegar and sugar for 1 minute.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Pour the hot pickled vegetables over.
> Serve with rice and peas.

# Jamaican Escovitch Chicken Breast
Main | Jamaican | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 1 | carrot, cut into thin strips
- 1 | red bell pepper, cut into thin strips
- 120 ml | white vinegar
- 1 tsp | ground allspice
- 1 tsp | sugar
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves
- 4 | spring onions, chopped
- 1 | scotch bonnet pepper, seeded
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and the allspice.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion, garlic, spring onions and scotch bonnet for 1 minute, then the onion, carrot, pepper and scotch bonnet for 2 minutes, then the vinegar and sugar for 1 minute.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Pour the hot pickled vegetables over.
> Serve with rice and peas.

# Cuban Ropa Vieja-Style Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Cuban | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 2 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 120 ml | dry white wine
- 60 g | green olives
- 2 | bay leaves
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 1 | green bell pepper, chopped
- 5 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the cumin and oregano.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, pepper and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the tomatoes, wine, bay leaves and 300 ml water; shred the meat at the end, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the olives. Taste for salt.
> Serve with white rice and black beans.

# Cuban Ropa Vieja-Style Chicken Drumsticks Stew
Main | Cuban | 20 | 68 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 2 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 120 ml | dry white wine
- 60 g | green olives
- 2 | bay leaves
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 1 | green bell pepper, chopped
- 5 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken drumsticks dry and season well with salt and the cumin and oregano.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken drumsticks in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, pepper and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the tomatoes, wine, bay leaves and 300 ml water; shred the meat at the end, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the olives. Taste for salt.
> Serve with white rice and black beans.

# Roast Cuban Mojo Chicken Thigh
Main | Cuban | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 | oranges, juiced
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 1 | onion, sliced into rings
- 1 | green bell pepper, chopped
- 5 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the citrus juice, cumin, oregano and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay in a roasting dish on the onion rings.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Spoon over the pan juices.
> Serve with white rice and black beans.

# Cuban Mojo Chicken Thigh
Main | Cuban | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 | oranges, juiced
- 1 | lime, juiced
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 1 | green bell pepper, chopped
- 5 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the cumin and oregano.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion, pepper and garlic for 1 minute, then the garlic for 1 minute, then the citrus juice; bubble 2 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Spoon the garlicky mojo over.
> Serve with white rice and black beans.

# Cuban Picadillo Chicken Mince
Main | Cuban | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 400 g | tomato sauce
- 60 g | green olives, sliced
- 50 g | raisins
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tbsp | capers
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 1 | green bell pepper, chopped
- 5 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the onion, pepper and garlic for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the cumin and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the tomato sauce, olives, raisins and capers and simmer 15 minutes.
> Serve with rice and fried plantains.

# Peruvian Saltado Chicken Thigh Stir-Fry
Main | Peruvian | 15 | 14 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 1 | red onion, in wedges
- 2 | tomatoes, in wedges
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 2 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 1 | green chili (aji amarillo if you can find it)
- 400 g | french fries, cooked
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken thigh into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the soy sauce and vinegar with the tomatoes.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken thigh in two batches, about 4 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the onion wedges and chili with the onion and garlic for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Toss with the hot fries.
> Serve with rice.

# Peruvian Saltado Chicken Breast Stir-Fry
Main | Peruvian | 15 | 12 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 1 | red onion, in wedges
- 2 | tomatoes, in wedges
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 2 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 1 | green chili (aji amarillo if you can find it)
- 400 g | french fries, cooked
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 1 tsp | cornstarch
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Slice the chicken breast into thin strips (if not already), toss with the cornstarch and a pinch of salt and leave while you prep.
> For the sauce, stir together the soy sauce and vinegar with the tomatoes.
> Heat the oil in a wok or large frying pan over high heat until shimmering. Stir-fry the chicken breast in two batches, about 3 minutes per batch, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); tip onto a plate.
> Stir-fry the onion wedges and chili with the onion and garlic for 2 to 3 minutes.
> Return the chicken breast with any juices, pour in the sauce and toss for 1 minute until glossy and hot. Toss with the hot fries.
> Serve with rice.

# Roast Peruvian Pollo a la Brasa-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Peruvian | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 | lime, juiced
- 2 tsp | ground cumin
- 2 tsp | paprika
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 1 | red onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the soy sauce, lime, cumin, paprika, oregano and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay on a rack over a lined tray.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with green sauce and fries.

# Peruvian Anticucho-Style Chicken Thigh Skewers
Main | Peruvian | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 2 tbsp | aji panca paste (or 2 tsp paprika and 1 tsp chili powder)
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 1 | red onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the vinegar, chili paste, cumin, oregano and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Thread on skewers and lay on a lined tray.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with boiled potatoes and corn.

# Peruvian Seco-Style Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Peruvian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 large handfuls | coriander
- 1 | green chili (aji amarillo if you can find it)
- 330 ml | beer
- 200 g | green peas
- 2 | carrots, diced
- 1 | red onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and ground cumin.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the coriander blended with the chili and beer, then the carrots for the last 30 minutes and the peas for the last 5, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve with rice and beans. Taste for salt.

# Brazilian Moqueca-Style Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Brazilian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 400 ml | coconut milk
- 2 tbsp | palm oil (or olive oil with 1 tsp paprika)
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced
- 3 | tomatoes, sliced
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 1 large handful | coriander
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the paprika.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the coconut milk, pepper and tomatoes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the lime juice and coriander. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice and black beans.

# Brazilian Moqueca-Style Chicken Drumsticks Stew
Main | Brazilian | 20 | 68 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 400 ml | coconut milk
- 2 tbsp | palm oil (or olive oil with 1 tsp paprika)
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced
- 3 | tomatoes, sliced
- 2 | limes, juiced
- 1 large handful | coriander
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken drumsticks dry and season well with salt and the paprika.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken drumsticks in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the coconut milk, pepper and tomatoes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the lime juice and coriander. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice and black beans.

# Brazilian Feijoada-Style Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Brazilian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 400 g | black beans, drained
- 200 g | smoked sausage, sliced
- 2 | bay leaves
- 500 ml | beef stock
- 1 | orange, sliced
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the beans, sausage, bay leaves and stock, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve with rice, greens and orange slices. Taste for salt.

# Brazilian Feijoada-Style Chicken Drumsticks Stew

# Grilled Brazilian Churrasco-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Brazilian | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tbsp | salt (coarse if you have it)
- 1 large handful | parsley
- 4 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 2 | tomatoes, finely chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the coarse salt and garlic only with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Mix the onion, tomatoes, parsley and vinegar as a vinaigrette salsa.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Slice and serve with the salsa.
> Serve with rice and black beans.

- 4 tbsp | red wine vinegar

> Roast for about 22 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.

# Grilled Brazilian Churrasco-Style Chicken Drumsticks
Main | Brazilian | 15 | 45 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks

- 4 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 2 | tomatoes, finely chopped

# Brazilian Acebolado Chicken Thigh
Main | Brazilian | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 | onions, sliced into rings
- 2 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 1 tsp | paprika
- 20 g | butter
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the paprika.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the onion rings in the butter until soft and golden, then the vinegar.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Pile the onions on top.
> Serve with rice and black beans.

# Brazilian Acebolado Chicken Breast
Main | Brazilian | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 2 | onions, sliced into rings
- 2 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 1 tsp | paprika
- 20 g | butter
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and the paprika.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the onion rings in the butter until soft and golden, then the vinegar.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Pile the onions on top.
> Serve with rice and black beans.

# Grilled Argentine Chimichurri Chicken Thigh
Main | Argentine | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 1 large handful | parsley, finely chopped
- 2 tsp | dried oregano
- 1 tsp | chili flakes
- 4 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 100 ml | olive oil
- 4 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix salt, a little of the oregano and the oil with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Mix the parsley, garlic, the rest of the oregano, chili, vinegar and oil as chimichurri.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Slice and spoon over the chimichurri.
> Serve with roast potatoes or a green salad.

# Argentine Salsa Criolla Chicken Thigh
Main | Argentine | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 1 | red onion, finely chopped
- 1 | red bell pepper, finely chopped
- 2 | tomatoes, finely chopped
- 4 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 4 tbsp | olive oil
- 4 | garlic cloves, minced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Meanwhile, off the heat, mix the onion, pepper, tomatoes, vinegar and oil for the salsa while the meat rests.
> Spoon the salsa over.
> Serve with roast potatoes or a green salad.

# Argentine Carbonada-Style Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Argentine | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 | sweet potatoes, in chunks
- 300 g | butternut squash, in chunks
- 2 | corn cobs, cut into rounds
- 2 | peaches (or dried apricots), chopped
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 500 ml | beef stock
- 4 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and paprika.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the tomatoes and stock, adding the vegetables and fruit for the last 30 minutes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve in bowls. Taste for salt.

# Italian Cacciatore Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | Italian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 150 ml | red wine
- 1 | red bell pepper, sliced
- 200 g | mushrooms, sliced
- 1 tsp | dried oregano
- 2 sprigs | rosemary
- 50 g | black olives
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the oregano and rosemary.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the wine (boil 1 minute), tomatoes, pepper and mushrooms, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the olives. Taste for salt.
> Serve with crusty bread, polenta or pasta.

# Italian Ragù Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | Italian | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 800 g | passata
- 150 ml | red wine
- 1 | carrot, finely chopped
- 1 | celery stalk, finely chopped
- 2 | bay leaves
- 400 g | pappardelle (or rigatoni)
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the wine, passata, carrot, celery and bay leaves; shred the meat at the end, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Toss with the pasta and serve with parmesan. Taste for salt.
> Serve with crusty bread, polenta or pasta.

# Roast Italian Porchetta-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | Italian | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tsp | fennel seeds, crushed
- 2 sprigs | rosemary, chopped
- 1 | lemon, zested
- 1 tsp | chili flakes
- 120 ml | dry white wine
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the fennel seeds, rosemary, lemon zest, chili and garlic with oil with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay in a roasting dish and pour the wine around.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Slice and spoon over the pan juices.
> Serve with crusty bread, polenta or pasta.

# Italian Piccata Chicken Thigh
Main | Italian | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 30 g | flour
- 1 | lemon, juiced
- 2 tbsp | capers
- 150 ml | chicken stock
- 30 g | butter
- 1 handful | parsley
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and black pepper; dust lightly with flour.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the stock, lemon juice and capers and bubble 2 minutes, then swirl in the butter.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Scatter with parsley.
> Serve with crusty bread, polenta or pasta.

# Italian Piccata Chicken Breast
Main | Italian | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 30 g | flour
- 1 | lemon, juiced
- 2 tbsp | capers
- 150 ml | chicken stock
- 30 g | butter
- 1 handful | parsley
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and black pepper; dust lightly with flour.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the stock, lemon juice and capers and bubble 2 minutes, then swirl in the butter.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Scatter with parsley.
> Serve with crusty bread, polenta or pasta.

# Italian Marsala Chicken Thigh
Main | Italian | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 30 g | flour
- 250 g | mushrooms, sliced
- 150 ml | Marsala wine (or dry sherry)
- 150 ml | chicken stock
- 30 g | butter
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and black pepper; dust lightly with flour.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the mushrooms in the butter until golden, then the Marsala and stock and reduce by half.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Spoon the mushrooms and sauce over.
> Serve with crusty bread, polenta or pasta.

# Italian Saltimbocca-Style Chicken Thigh

- 8 | sage leaves

- 20 g | butter
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced

# Italian Polpette Chicken Meatballs
Main | Italian | 20 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 1 | egg
- 50 g | breadcrumbs
- 40 g | parmesan, grated
- 1 handful | parsley, chopped
- 700 g | passata
- 1 handful | basil leaves
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the chicken with the egg, breadcrumbs, parmesan, parsley and half the garlic and 1 tsp salt; with wet hands shape into 16 balls.
> Brown in 2 tbsp oil over medium-high heat in batches, about 6 minutes, turning; they finish cooking in the sauce.
> Cook the onion and garlic gently in the same pan for 5 minutes.
> Add the passata and simmer the meatballs gently for 20 minutes, until the meat is no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Tear over the basil and serve with bread or pasta.
> Serve with crusty bread, polenta or pasta.

# Italian Bolognese-Style Chicken Ragù
Main | Italian | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 1 | carrot, finely chopped
- 1 | celery stalk, finely chopped
- 150 ml | red wine
- 700 g | passata
- 400 g | tagliatelle
- 40 g | parmesan, grated
- 1 | onion, finely chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the onion and garlic for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the carrot and celery and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the wine, then the passata; simmer 30 minutes.
> Toss with the pasta and parmesan.
> Serve with crusty bread, polenta or pasta.

# French Coq au Vin-Style Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | French | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 500 ml | red wine
- 150 g | bacon lardons
- 250 g | mushrooms
- 12 | pearl onions (or 2 onions in wedges)
- 2 sprigs | thyme
- 2 | bay leaves
- 2 tbsp | flour
- 2 | shallots, finely chopped
- 2 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the thyme and flour.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the shallots and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the wine, bay leaves and 250 ml stock or water; fry the bacon, mushrooms and onions separately and add for the last 20 minutes, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Scatter with parsley. Taste for salt.
> Serve with crusty bread, mashed potatoes or green beans.

# Roast French Dijon Herb Chicken Thigh
Main | French | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 tbsp | dijon mustard
- 2 tsp | herbes de Provence
- 40 g | breadcrumbs
- 20 g | butter, melted
- 2 | shallots, finely chopped
- 2 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the mustard, herbs and garlic with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay on a lined tray and press on the breadcrumbs mixed with butter.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with the pan juices.

# French Au Poivre Chicken Thigh
Main | French | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tbsp | black peppercorns, coarsely crushed
- 3 tbsp | brandy (optional)
- 150 ml | heavy cream
- 20 g | butter
- 2 | shallots, finely chopped
- 2 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the crushed peppercorns, pressed on both sides.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the shallots and garlic for 1 minute, then the brandy (careful, it can flame), then the cream; bubble 2 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Spoon the peppery cream over.
> Serve with crusty bread, mashed potatoes or green beans.

# Spanish Chorizo and Chickpea Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Spanish | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 150 g | chorizo, sliced
- 400 g | chickpeas, drained
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 2 tsp | smoked paprika
- 200 g | spinach
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the smoked paprika.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the chorizo, tomatoes, chickpeas and 300 ml water, with the spinach at the end, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Drizzle with olive oil. Taste for salt.
> Serve with crusty bread or potatoes.

# Spanish Pimentón Chicken Thigh Traybake
Main | Spanish | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tsp | smoked paprika
- 1 | lemon, juiced
- 600 g | potatoes, cubed
- 1 | red bell pepper, in chunks
- 1 | red onion, in wedges
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the smoked paprika, lemon juice, garlic and oil with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Spread on a tray with the potatoes, pepper and onion (start the potatoes 15 minutes earlier).
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Scatter with parsley.
> Serve with crusty bread or potatoes.

# Spanish Pimentón Chicken Breast Traybake

- 600 g | potatoes, cubed
- 1 | red bell pepper, in chunks
- 1 | red onion, in wedges
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the smoked paprika, lemon juice, garlic and oil with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken breast and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to 4 hours in the fridge.

> Roast for about 22 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Scatter with parsley.
> Serve with crusty bread or potatoes.

# Spanish Al Ajillo Chicken Thigh
Main | Spanish | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 6 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 1 tsp | chili flakes
- 100 ml | dry white wine
- 1 handful | parsley
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the sliced garlic and chili gently until golden, then the wine.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Scatter with parsley and serve with bread.
> Serve with crusty bread or potatoes.

# Spanish Albondigas en Salsa Chicken Meatballs
Main | Spanish | 20 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 1 | egg
- 50 g | breadcrumbs
- 1 tsp | smoked paprika
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 100 ml | dry white wine
- 1 handful | parsley
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, sliced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the chicken with the egg, breadcrumbs, paprika and half the garlic and 1 tsp salt; with wet hands shape into 16 balls.
> Brown in 2 tbsp oil over medium-high heat in batches, about 6 minutes, turning; they finish cooking in the sauce.
> Cook the onion and garlic gently in the same pan for 5 minutes.
> Add the wine and tomatoes and simmer the meatballs 20 minutes, until the meat is no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Scatter with parsley.
> Serve with crusty bread or potatoes.

# Portuguese Alentejana-Style Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | Portuguese | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 1 tbsp | paprika
- 1 tbsp | tomato paste
- 250 ml | dry white wine
- 2 | bay leaves
- 500 g | clams (optional)
- 1 handful | coriander
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the paprika.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the wine and bay leaves; add the clams for the last 5 minutes until they open, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Scatter with coriander. Taste for salt.
> Serve with potatoes or rice.

# Portuguese Alentejana-Style Braised Chicken Drumsticks

> Add the wine and bay leaves; add the clams for the last 5 minutes until they open, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).

# Portuguese Garlic Wine Chicken Thigh Bitoque-Style
Main | Portuguese | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 150 ml | dry white wine
- 2 | bay leaves
- 4 | eggs, fried
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 4 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the garlic and bay leaves, then the wine; bubble 2 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Serve each portion topped with a fried egg.

# Greek Stifado Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | Greek | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 500 g | shallots, peeled
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 150 ml | red wine
- 2 tbsp | red wine vinegar
- 1 | cinnamon stick
- 4 | cloves
- 2 | bay leaves
- 1 | red onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the cinnamon and cloves.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the wine, vinegar, tomatoes, bay leaves and whole shallots, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve with rice or orzo. Taste for salt.

# Greek Lemon Oregano Chicken Thighs Lemonato
Main | Greek | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 | lemons, juiced
- 2 tsp | dried oregano
- 600 g | potatoes, in wedges
- 250 ml | chicken stock
- 1 | red onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the oregano.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.

> Simmer uncovered until the sauce is reduced and lemony. Taste for salt.
> Serve with rice, potatoes or warm pitta.

# Greek Souvlaki Chicken Thigh Skewers

- 2 tsp | dried oregano

- 1 | cucumber, grated

- 1 | red onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed

> Rest 5 minutes. Serve in warm pitta with the tzatziki.

# Turkish Pepper Paste Chicken Thighs Güveç-Style
Main | Turkish | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 tbsp | tomato paste (or Turkish red pepper paste)
- 2 | green bell peppers, sliced
- 3 | tomatoes, chopped
- 1 | eggplant, cubed
- 1 tsp | Aleppo pepper (or chili flakes)
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the pepper paste and Aleppo pepper.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the tomatoes, eggplant, peppers and 300 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve with yogurt. Taste for salt.

# Moroccan Apricot Chicken Thighs Tagine
Main | Moroccan | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 tsp | ras el hanout
- 1 tsp | ground cinnamon
- 150 g | dried apricots
- 400 g | chickpeas, drained
- 1 tbsp | honey
- 30 g | almonds, toasted
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the ras el hanout and cinnamon.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the apricots, chickpeas, honey and 500 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Scatter with almonds. Taste for salt.
> Serve with couscous.

# Moroccan Preserved Lemon and Olive Chicken Thighs Tagine
Main | Moroccan | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 2 | preserved lemons, chopped (or 1 lemon, zested and juiced)
- 100 g | green olives
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 1 tsp | ground ginger
- 1 large handful | coriander
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 cm | ginger, grated

> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the turmeric and ginger.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the preserved lemon and 400 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the olives and coriander. Taste for salt.
> Serve with couscous.

# Moroccan Preserved Lemon and Olive Chicken Drumsticks Tagine
Main | Moroccan | 20 | 68 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 2 | preserved lemons, chopped (or 1 lemon, zested and juiced)
- 100 g | green olives
- 1 tsp | turmeric
- 1 tsp | ground ginger
- 1 large handful | coriander
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken drumsticks dry and season well with salt and the turmeric and ginger.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken drumsticks in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion, garlic and ginger for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the preserved lemon and 400 ml water, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 45 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat is tender and pulls from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the olives and coriander. Taste for salt.
> Serve with couscous.

# Roast Moroccan Chermoula Chicken Thigh
Main | Moroccan | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 1 large handful | coriander, chopped
- 1 large handful | parsley, chopped
- 2 tsp | ground cumin
- 2 tsp | paprika
- 1 | lemon, juiced
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the herbs, cumin, paprika, lemon and garlic blended with oil with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Lay on a lined tray.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with lemon wedges.

# Roast Moroccan Chermoula Chicken Breast

# Roast Moroccan Chermoula Chicken Drumsticks
Main | Moroccan | 15 | 45 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken drumsticks
- 1 large handful | coriander, chopped
- 1 large handful | parsley, chopped
- 2 tsp | ground cumin
- 2 tsp | paprika
- 1 | lemon, juiced
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the herbs, cumin, paprika, lemon and garlic blended with oil with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken drumsticks and leave to marinate 1 hour or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 210 °C (190 °C fan). Lay on a lined tray.

# Moroccan Kefta Chicken Tagine
Main | Moroccan | 20 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 1 tsp | ground cumin
- 1 tsp | paprika
- 1 handful | parsley, chopped
- 400 g | chopped tomatoes
- 4 | eggs
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, crushed
- 2 cm | ginger, grated
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the chicken with the cumin, paprika, parsley and half the onion and 1 tsp salt; with wet hands shape into 8 patties or logs.
> Brown in 2 tbsp oil over medium-high heat in batches, about 6 minutes, turning; they finish cooking in the sauce.
> Cook the onion, garlic and ginger gently in the same pan for 5 minutes.
> Add the tomatoes; simmer the meatballs 15 minutes, then crack in the eggs, cover and cook 6 minutes, until the meat is no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve with bread.

# American Pot Roast-Style Braised Chicken Thighs
Main | American | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 3 | carrots, in chunks
- 600 g | potatoes, in chunks
- 2 tbsp | tomato paste
- 500 ml | beef stock
- 2 sprigs | thyme
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the thyme and tomato paste.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the stock, with the carrots and potatoes for the last hour, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Thicken the gravy if you like and serve. Taste for salt.
> Serve with mashed potatoes, corn or a green salad.

# American BBQ Chicken Thigh Traybake
Main | American | 15 | 30 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 150 ml | barbecue sauce
- 1 tbsp | smoked paprika
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 tsp | garlic powder
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Mix the paprika, sugar, garlic powder and half the sauce with 2 tbsp oil and 1 tsp salt. Coat the chicken thigh and leave to marinate 30 minutes or up to overnight in the fridge.
> Heat the oven to 200 °C (180 °C fan). Spread on a lined tray, brushing with the rest of the sauce for the last 10 minutes.
> Roast for about 25 minutes, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F); for a darker finish, grill the top for the last 2 to 3 minutes.
> Rest 5 minutes. Serve with coleslaw.

# Roast American Maple Mustard Chicken Thigh

- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 3 tbsp | maple syrup
- 2 tbsp | dijon mustard
- 1 tbsp | cider vinegar
- 2 sprigs | rosemary
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced

# American Honey Garlic Chicken Thigh
Main | American | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 4 tbsp | honey
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | cider vinegar
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the garlic for 30 seconds, then the honey, soy sauce and vinegar; bubble until sticky.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Turn to coat.
> Serve with mashed potatoes, corn or a green salad.

# American Honey Garlic Chicken Breast
Main | American | 10 | 20 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 700 g | chicken breasts
- 4 tbsp | honey
- 3 tbsp | soy sauce
- 1 tbsp | cider vinegar
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken breast dry and season with salt and black pepper.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Butterfly or pound to an even 2 cm, then cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the garlic for 30 seconds, then the honey, soy sauce and vinegar; bubble until sticky.
> Return the chicken breast with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Turn to coat.
> Serve with mashed potatoes, corn or a green salad.

# American Creamy Tuscan-Style Chicken Thigh
Main | American | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 100 g | sun-dried tomatoes, chopped
- 100 g | spinach
- 200 ml | heavy cream
- 40 g | parmesan, grated
- 1 tsp | Italian seasoning
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the Italian seasoning.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the sun-dried tomatoes and garlic, then the cream and parmesan, then the spinach until wilted.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Spoon over.
> Serve with mashed potatoes, corn or a green salad.

# American Sloppy Joe-Style Chicken Sandwiches
Main | American | 10 | 25 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 600 g | ground chicken
- 200 g | ketchup
- 1 tbsp | Worcestershire sauce
- 1 tbsp | brown sugar
- 1 | green bell pepper, diced
- 4 | burger buns
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 3 | garlic cloves, minced
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat and cook the onion and garlic for 5 minutes.
> Add the chicken and the pepper and cook, breaking it up, for 6 to 8 minutes until no longer pink inside (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Stir in the ketchup, Worcestershire sauce, sugar and 100 ml water and simmer 10 minutes.
> Pile onto toasted buns.
> Serve with mashed potatoes, corn or a green salad.

# British Ale Chicken Thighs Stew
Main | British | 20 | 63 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 1.2 kg | chicken thighs, bone-in, skin-on
- 440 ml | beer (a brown ale)
- 2 | carrots, in chunks
- 2 tbsp | flour
- 1 tbsp | Worcestershire sauce
- 2 sprigs | thyme
- 2 | bay leaves
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 2 | garlic cloves
- 2 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thighs dry and season well with salt and the flour and thyme.
> Heat 2 tbsp oil in a heavy pot over medium-high heat and brown the chicken thighs in batches, about 8 minutes in total; set aside.
> Lower the heat and cook the onion and garlic for 6 to 8 minutes until soft.
> Add the ale, Worcestershire sauce, bay leaves, carrots and 200 ml stock, scrape up the browned bits and return the meat. Cover and simmer gently for about 40 minutes (or bake covered at 160 °C), until the meat pulls easily from the bone and cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F).
> Serve with mash or crusty bread. Taste for salt.

# British Peppercorn Chicken Thigh
Main | British | 10 | 18 | 4 | Dinner, Main dish
- 800 g | chicken thighs, boneless and skinless
- 2 tsp | black pepper, coarsely crushed
- 150 ml | double cream
- 1 tsp | dijon mustard
- 1 | onion, chopped
- 2 | garlic cloves
- 1 tbsp | vegetable oil
> Pat the chicken thigh dry and season with salt and the crushed peppercorns.
> Heat 1 tbsp oil in a large frying pan over medium-high heat. Cook 5 to 6 minutes per side, until cooked through, with no pink in the middle (74 °C / 165 °F). Rest on a warm plate.
> Lower the heat and, in the same pan, cook the onion and garlic for 1 minute, then the cream and mustard; bubble 2 minutes.
> Return the chicken thigh with its resting juices and turn in the sauce. Spoon over and serve with chips or salad.
> Serve with mashed potatoes and greens.
